Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on geology structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 79. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 121 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by geology generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 79, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 121. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The geology variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 767 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 542 and intersection form of rank 5.
